15:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L16",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-10-06","generated_at":"2026-10-17T06:41:28.183237+00:00","stats":{"total":170,"starred":0,"must_read":16,"skim":93,"glance":61,"by_category":{"pretraining_data":3,"llm_pretrain":9,"scaling":1,"architecture":12,"efficient":23,"eval":24,"multimodal":10,"multimodal_gen":15,"post_train":23,"mechanism":21,"coding_agent":6,"agent":23},"fetch_status":{"arxiv":"ok","huggingface":"ok"}},"categories":[{"id":"pretraining_data","emoji":"📚","name":"预训练数据","description":"以预训练数据为核心贡献的工作：数据采集/清洗/去重、数据配比(data mixture)、数据筛选/质量评估、语料构建、数据课程(data curriculum)、数据重复(repetition)、合成数据用于预训练、Tokenizer/词表设计、成员推断/数据检测。判据是『论文核心是数据本身』，而非训练 recipe/优化器(→ llm_pretrain)或评测资产(→ eval)","papers":[{"id":"2610.02593","title":"Fisher-Guided Submodular Data Selection for Continual Pre-Training of Large Language Models","abstract":"Data selection is already a central bottleneck in large-language-model training, where web-scale corpora are noisy and token budgets are finite. In continual pre-training (CPT), it becomes a forgetting-control problem: a poorly chosen target-domain corpus can overwrite capabilities encoded in the pretrained checkpoint. Existing CPT practice either scores candidates with parameter-agnostic scalars such as perplexity, or mitigates forgetting by spending many extra general-domain replay tokens. Nei","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-05","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2610_02593.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解决 continual pre-training 中目标域数据选择不当导致灾难性遗忘的问题，尤其是如何在有限 token 预算下适配新域而不过度覆盖原有能力。","importance":"这是一篇数据选择方向的机制化工作。价值不在于又做了一个打分器，而在于把 CPT 遗忘和参数空间中的 Fisher 重要性联系起来，给出比 perplexity 或大规模 replay 更贴近模型内部状态的选择准则。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Fisher-aware 的子模数据选择。论文先观察到：基于 loss 的样本选择会让 CPT 后模型在预训练阶段高 Fisher 坐标上的 Fisher 对角值明显下滑，说明模型原本“承诺”过的重要参数被冲淡。基于这个现象，作者把候选样本的梯度分解到高/低 Fisher 坐标上，用子模优化挑选既能推动目标域学习、又尽量少扰动高 Fisher 参数的样本子集，从而在不依赖大量通用域 replay 的情况下控制遗忘。","experiments":"从摘要可见，论文比较了 Fisher-aware 选择与基于 perplexity/loss 的选择，以及依赖额外通用域 replay 的常见 CPT 做法。重点实验应围绕：目标域适应效果、原有通用能力保留、以及 Fisher 对角漂移分析。摘要没有给出模型规模和具体数据量，能确认的是作者强调在有限监督 token 预算下取得更好的适应-保留折中，并用 Fisher drift 作为机制证据。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles data selection for continual pre-training, aiming to adapt a pretrained LLM to a target domain without overwriting capabilities already stored in the checkpoint under a limited token budget.","importance":"This is a mechanism-oriented data selection paper. Its value is not merely another scoring heuristic, but the link it draws between catastrophic forgetting in CPT and parameter importance measured by the Fisher diagonal, offering a more model-aware criterion than perplexity or brute-force replay.","technical_approach":"The method is a Fisher-guided submodular selector. The paper first shows that loss-based selection drives the post-CPT Fisher diagonal downward specifically on coordinates that had high Fisher under the pretrained model, suggesting that important parameters are being overwritten. It then decomposes each candidate example's gradient with respect to high- and low-Fisher coordinates, and uses submodular optimization to pick examples that improve target-domain learning while minimally perturbing high-Fisher parameters, reducing reliance on large amounts of general-domain replay.","experiments":"From the abstract, the comparisons include Fisher-aware selection versus perplexity/loss-based selection and replay-heavy CPT baselines. This makes expected return a poor fit for compositional reasoning, where a solution must be ass","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-05","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_02478.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解决 LLM 强化学习里“期望回报”目标会把多条部分正确但分散出现的推理轨迹压扁，导致组合式推理难以积累的问题。","importance":"这是一个目标函数层面的改写，不是常规 reward shaping。它试图修正 RL for reasoning 中“成功轨迹求和”带来的遗忘和信用分配问题，对长链推理、可验证任务和 R1 类训练范式有一定启发。","technical_approach":"作者提出 Tropical Reinforcement Learning，把传统 Bellman/return 聚合中的加法替换为 tropical semiring 上的 max。这样状态价值不再表示所有成功轨迹概率之和，而是“最可能被验证成功的那条路径”的对数概率，并保留一条可回放、可复用的显式解路径。论文的核心主张是：对组合式推理而言，max-over-solutions 比 sum-over-solutions 更贴近训练目标，因为不同尝试中出现的局部正确步骤可以通过显式路径重用而不是被概率归一化互相挤掉。","experiments":"摘要没有给出完整规模细节，但实验重点应是 reasoning/RL 场景下与标准 expected-return RL 的比较，关注是否能减少对未观察到正确组合的遗忘，并提升可验证推理任务的成功率。当前从摘要看，更像概念验证而非大规模工业级训练报告，结论需要看具体任务、基线和 ablation 是否干净。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"It addresses a mismatch in RL for LLMs where expected-return objectives collapse dispersed partially correct reasoning trajectories, making compositional reasoning hard to accumulate.","importance":"This is an objective-level proposal rather than routine reward shaping. As model size and context grow, write-conflict in long-term memory and KV bandwidth pressure become more severe, so this inductive bias should become more—not less—useful. The paper already reports the same direction from 150M to 1B, suggesting the effect is not a one-off size artifact. If proxy runs further show stable loss ordering across scales, stronger later-pass attention concentration on shared KV, and increasing specialization of pass roles, rank reversal risk is low."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证“训练期共享记忆归纳偏置”是否在等算力下稳定改善预训练，并确认收益来自跨轮分工而非参数量或cache技巧。","en":"Validate whether shared-memory recurrence is a robust pretraining inductive bias under iso-FLOPs, and confirm the gain comes from cross-pass specialization rather than parameter count or cache tricks."},"data_sources":{"zh":"FineWeb-Edu或同类高质量英文web语料；模型梯度阶梯设为约150M、400M、1.0B三档；上下文长度统一如4K或8K；严格Iso-FLOPs匹配总训练token。比较三组：标准Transformer、纯局部KV循环模型、共享主KV+小本地窗口的hybrid循环模型。循环次数先固定5。","en":"Use FineWeb-Edu or a comparable high-quality English web corpus. Secondary signals: later-pass attention mass on shared global KV, cross-pass hidden-state CKA specialization, and loss improvement per unit KV memory."},"success_criteria":{"zh":"成功：共享记忆hybrid在三档尺度上都优于标准Transformer与纯局部循环，验证CE相对标准Transformer至少改善0.5%/0.7%/1.0%（随尺度不下降），且相对纯局部循环至少改善0.3%；训练时共享、推理时共享的伪对照不能复现同等收益；机制上后续轮次对共享KV的attention占比显著高于首轮后写入版本。证伪：收益只出现在最小尺度，或一旦严格Iso-FLOPs后优势消失，或伪对照同样拿到大部分收益。","en":"Success: the shared-memory hybrid beats both the standard Transformer and local-KV looped baseline at all three scales, improving validation CE over the standard Transformer by at least 0.5%/0.7%/1.0% (non-decreasing with scale) and by at least 0.3% over local-KV recurrence; the inference-only KV-sharing pseudo-control should not recover the same gain; mechanistically, later passes should place substantially more attention mass on shared KV than variants that keep writing after pass one. Falsification: gains appear only at the smallest scale, disappear under strict iso-FLOPs, or are mostly reproduced by the pseudo-control."},"compute_tier":"medium"},"risk":{"zh":"若循环实现暗中增加了有效深度、参数复用方式或优化稳定性，可能把收益误归因给共享记忆；另一个风险是长上下文收益在短上下文proxy上被低估。","en":"If the recurrence implementation implicitly changes effective depth, parameter reuse, or optimization stability, the gain may be misattributed to shared memory. Another risk is that short-context proxy runs underestimate benefits that emerge mainly in longer contexts."},"source_paper_ids":["2610.02383"],"scaling_confidence":0.82},{"pillar":"pretrain_data","title":{"zh":"Fisher约束选数降低持续预训练遗忘","en":"Fisher-constrained data selection reduces continual-pretrain forgetting"},"claim":{"zh":"在持续预训练中，用样本梯度对高Fisher参数坐标的扰动作为约束做子模选数，比按loss/perplexity选数更能保持原有通用能力，同时保留大部分目标域适应收益；关键机制是避免冲淡模型已承诺的重要参数。","en":"In continual pretraining, submodular data selection that constrains sample-induced updates on high-Fisher coordinates will preserve general-domain capability better than loss/perplexity-based selection while retaining most target-domain adaptation gains, because it avoids washing out parameters to which the model is already strongly committed."},"why_scales":{"zh":"这不是依赖小模型容量不足的现象，而是和参数重要性几何有关。持续预训练的遗忘来自更新方向与既有功能子空间冲突；Fisher对角近似虽然粗糙，但在大模型里通常更稳定，因为高频、跨分布复用的功能会在更多参数上留下一致的重要性信号。随着规模增大，单纯按loss选数更容易偏向“最能立刻降目标域loss”的样本，从而过度利用可塑参数并侵蚀共享能力；Fisher约束相当于在数据层做近似EWC，机制不会因模型变大而消失。它也不依赖特定领域，只依赖base模型已有知识结构，因此外推到更大基座的把握较高。","en":"This is not a small-model capacity artifact; it is about parameter-importance geometry. Forgetting in continual pretraining arises when update directions interfere with subspaces supporting existing capabilities. Although diagonal Fisher is approximate, it often becomes more stable in larger models because frequently reused, cross-domain functions leave more consistent importance signals across parameters. As scale grows, pure loss-based selection is even more likely to over-prefer samples that immediately reduce target-domain loss, overusing plastic directions and eroding shared capability. Fisher-constrained selection is effectively a data-level analogue of approximate EWC, so the mechanism should persist rather than vanish with scale. It is also domain-agnostic, relying on the base model's existing knowledge structure rather than a narrow task setup."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证Fisher-aware选数是否在小到中等规模CPT中稳定改善“适应-保留”前沿，并确认Fisher drift是可操作的早期代理指标。","en":"Validate whether Fisher-aware selection consistently improves the adaptation-retention frontier in small-to-mid-scale continual pretraining, and whether Fisher drift is a usable early proxy metric."},"data_sources":{"zh":"先训一个通用base（如150M、600M、1.3B三档，统一在通用web+code混合语料上预训练到同等困惑度区间），再做CPT到两个目标域：代码强化域与专业文本域（如数学/科学文档）。每个目标域准备大候选池，但CPT token预算固定。对照包括：随机采样、按loss/perplexity选数、少量通用replay、Fisher-aware子模选择。","en":"Pretrain a general base at three scales (e.g., 150M, 600M, 1.3B) on a common web+code mixture to comparable perplexity. Then run continual pretraining into two target domains: a code-heavy domain and a specialized text domain (e.g., math/science documents). For each target domain, prepare a large candidate pool but fix the CPT token budget. Compare random sampling, loss/perplexity-based selection, small general-domain replay, and Fisher-aware submodular selection."},"steps":[{"zh":"步骤1：在base结束点估计对角Fisher，定义高Fisher坐标集合；对候选样本计算梯度在高/低Fisher子空间上的投影统计，并在固定选数预算下做子模挑选。与loss/perplexity排序选数保持相同token预算。","en":"Step 1: Estimate diagonal Fisher at the base checkpoint and define a high-Fisher coordinate set. For each candidate sample, compute gradient projections onto high- and low-Fisher subspaces, then perform submodular selection under a fixed token budget. Match the token budget exactly to loss/perplexity-based selection."},{"zh":"步骤2：在三档尺度和两个目标域上做CPT，并在训练早期、中期、结束点测量通用域验证CE、目标域验证CE、知识保留基准、以及高Fisher坐标的Fisher drift。再加一个“Fisher-aware+极少量replay”组合，检查是否存在可加性。","en":"Step 2: Run CPT across the three scales and two target domains, measuring general-domain validation CE, target-domain validation CE, retention benchmarks, and Fisher drift on high-Fisher coordinates at early, mid, and final checkpoints. Add a 'Fisher-aware + tiny replay' condition to test additivity."}],"eval_signal":{"zh":"核心信号是固定目标域收益下的通用域损失回退，或固定通用域回退下的目标域收益；早期代理信号是高Fisher坐标的对角Fisher下降幅度、梯度与高Fisher子空间夹角、以及CPT后通用域子集条件交叉熵。","en":"Core signal: general-domain regression at fixed target-domain gain, or target-domain gain at fixed general-domain regression. Early proxy signals: drop in diagonal Fisher on high-Fisher coordinates, gradient angle to the high-Fisher subspace, and post-CPT conditional cross-entropy on a held-out general-domain subset."},"success_criteria":{"zh":"成功：在至少2/3模型尺度、且两个目标域中都成立——Fisher-aware相对loss/perplexity选数，在目标域验证CE不差于其1%以内时，将通用域CE回退降低至少15%；或在相同通用域回退下，目标域CE再改善至少1%。同时高Fisher drift在训练前10-20% token内就能预测最终保留表现，Spearman相关≥0.7。证伪：收益只在单一领域成立，或一旦加入少量replay后Fisher-aware不再提供额外前沿改善。","en":"Success: in at least 2 of 3 model scales and in both target domains, Fisher-aware selection reduces general-domain CE regression by at least 15% relative to loss/perplexity selection while staying within 1% of its target-domain validation CE; alternatively, at matched general-domain regression it improves target-domain CE by at least 1%. In addition, high-Fisher drift within the first 10-20% of CPT tokens should predict final retention with Spearman correlation >= 0.7. Falsification: gains hold only in one domain, or vanish once a small replay buffer is added."},"compute_tier":"small"},"risk":{"zh":"对角Fisher近似可能过粗，尤其在大词表embedding/head主导的更新里；若目标域与通用域高度一致，遗忘本来就弱，方法优势会被压缩。","en":"Diagonal Fisher may be too crude, especially when updates are dominated by large-vocabulary embedding/head parameters. If the target domain is already close to the general domain, forgetting is weak and the method's advantage may be compressed."},"source_paper_ids":["2610.02593"],"scaling_confidence":0.79}]},"dates":"$9:0:props:dates","currentDate":"2026-10-06"}]}]}]
